# 26020-Package_Requirements

# West Doe Combined Equipment Package Basic Scope Descriptions

| Use: This document is a package-by-package scope, interface, and vendor deliverables coordination reference for EPC integration of package-vendor scopes. |
| --- |

# 26020-01-PT-12-001 - Acid Gas Compressor

| Location / Status | 4-25 West Doe Deepcut; budgetary RFQ basis; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-01-PT-RFQ-12-001_Acid Gas Compressor.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply Acid Gas Injection (AGI) compressor packages consisting of two (2x) identical induction motor-driven separable reciprocating compressor packages; each designed for 100% of acid gas capacity. Process function: Acid gases (H2S & CO2) coming out of the Amine treating will be compressed and sent to injection wells for storage.

## Major Included Equipment

Ariel KBT/6, 5 stage reciprocating compressors with Meg Driver motor; Compressor motor: 969 KW (1300HP) with VFD, 4000V/3PH/60HZ,900 rpm.; Air Cooler; forced air Intercooler after each stage of compression. Mounted on common frame, API-661 (modified). 1x36.8 KW (50HP) fan motor. Warm air recirculation plenum heater.; 1st Stage Scrubber - Two phase c/w Cyclonic Element. Vendor to design Size and Capacity; 2nd/3rd/4th/5th Stage Scrubber - Two phase c/w Demister. Vendor to design Size and Capacity.; Stage 1 Scrubber Liquid Pump - 0.75 HP Hydra-Cell Pump. 2x100%. Seal Less pump.; Packing vent/drain separation pot - Two phase, DP: 101 Kpag, Vendor to design size & capacity.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Shipping compressor packages to site, installation on piles, tie-in piping, electrical connections. Mounting platform and stairs. Capacity/design throughput: 4.209 MMSCFD (approx.), 3:1 turndown ratio. Driver: Electric Induction motor: 969 KW (1300HP) with VFD, 4000V/3PH/60HZ,900 rpm, TEFC, Non-spring bidirectional cooling fan, 3:1 speed turndown ratio. Operating conditions: Normal operating suction pressure- 1at stage 48 Kpag (& Psig); discharge pressure 5th stage 8274 Kpag (1200Psig). Target 1:10 compression capacity. Design conditions: MAWP-1st stage/2nd stage 1400 Kpag (205 Psig); 3rd stage 3565 Kpag (517 Psig); 4th stage 7129 Kpag (1034 Psig); 5th stage 10694 Kpag (1551 Psig). Design Temp. suction 149 C, discharge 177 C. |
| --- |

# 26020-01-PT-12-002 - Vapour Recovery Unit

| Location / Status | 4-25 West Doe Deepcut; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/_Budgetary/26020-01-PT-RFQ-12-002_VRU_2_R0.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply two (2) complete 100% capacity VRU compressor packages in a lead-lag configuration for sour service, both housed in one building. Each train includes a two-stage Ro-Flo 17S/217M positive displacement rotary vane compressor driven by a 200 HP VFD motor.

## Major Included Equipment

Compressors (1st & 2nd Stage); Quantity: 4 total (1st and 2nd stage for Train 1; 1st and 2nd stage for Train 2). 2 x 100% capacity.; Type: Ro-Flo 17S/217M two-stage rotary vane, positive displacement.; Service: Sour service (0.3557 mol% H₂S. 0.9434 mol% CO2), NACE designation applies.; Seals: Dual mechanical pressurized barrier seal (Plan 53 type) using fuel gas, with alarms and primary vent to LP flare.; Motors; Quantity: 2 total (1 per train). Stage 1 and 2 of each train will be driven by the same motor

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Shipping compressor packages to site, installation on piles, tie-in piping, electrical connections. Mounting platform and stairs etc. Capacity/design throughput: Stage 1; Expected flow summer capacity 0.5794 MMSCFD; Expected flow Winter capacity 0.4213 MMSCFD; Stage 2; Expected flow summer capacity 0.5638 MMSCFD; Expected flow Winter capacity 0.4182 MMSCFD. Driver: 200 HP/ 600V / 3 phase/ 60 Hz for Compressors, VFD ready.; All cooler motors also VFD ready and 600V. Operating conditions: 1st stage intercooler outlet Temp (operating) 48.9 C; 2nd stage aftercooler outlet Temp (operating) 60.0 C; 1st stage dewpoint Temp 45.6 C; 2nd stage dewpoint Temp 53.2 C. Design conditions: 0.9 kPag (2 oz/in2) design suction pressure; 483 kPag (70 psig) design discharge pressure; 42 e3m3/d (1.5 MMSCFD) design capacity; Design Temp 102 C. |
| --- |

# 26020-01-PT-12-003 - Inlet / Sales Compressors

| Location / Status | 4-25 West Doe Deepcut; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/RFQ/Bid Docs/26020-01-PT-RFQ-12-003-Inlet Sales Comp.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply Five (5) inlet/sales gas compressors Ariel KBC/6, 3 stage reciprocating compressors reciprocating compressor driven by a DOL driver with a soft-start. Each compressor shall be sized for 120%. Inlet gas is single stage and sales gas is double stage.

## Major Included Equipment

Five (5) inlet/sales gas compressors Ariel KBC/6, 3 stage reciprocating compressors reciprocating compressor driven by a DOL driver with a soft-start. Each compressor shall.; Compressor 8 Pole Induction Motor rated for 5,000 kW (6,700 HP) per unit (includes ~10% excess at design), rated 6600V/3PH/60Hz, 891 RPM.; Suction scrubbers: two-phase, upstream of each stage; vertical flow vane-style demisters (horizontal and vertical acceptable); K factor <= 0.5 Imperial plus de-ration for pressure; liquid density basis 0.61 SG; One common air cooler frame per package for both services; automated louver control via pneumatic temperature control on each process bundle

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Shipping compressor package to site, installation on piles, tie-in piping, electrical connections. Mounting platform and stairs. Capacity/design throughput: Design capacity:; Inlet comp 1,766 e3m3/d (62.4 MMSCFD) design capacity (each); Sales comp 1,630 e3m3/d (57.6 MMSCFD) design capacity (each); No turndown. Driver: Electric induction motor, 8-pole, 5,220 kW (7,000 HP) per unit (includes ~10% excess at design), rated 6600V/3PH/60Hz, 891 RPM. TEFC or WPII, non-sparking bidirectional cooling fan. NEMA MG 1; Total connected load (5 units)** \| 26,100 kW (35,000 HP); KBZ Frame, 6700hp, preferred WEG motor. Operating conditions: 4,309 kPag (625 psig) suction pressure; 7,791 kPag (1,130 psig) discharge pressure / 3,034 kPag (440 psig) suction pressure; 10,343 kPag (1,500 psig) discharge pressure. Design conditions: 1,766 e3m3/d (62.4 MMSCFD) design capacity (each) / 1,630 e3m3/d (57.6 MMSCFD) design capacity (each). |

# 26020-01-PT-12-004 - Sales Gas Booster Compressor

| Location / Status | 4-25 West Doe Deepcut; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | RFQ/Bid Docs/26020-01-PT-RFQ-12-004-Sales Booster Comp.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply Sales gas booster compressor package consisting of a motor driven separable, reciprocating gas pump package and a filter coalescer. Process function: Sweet Sales Gas comes from sales gas booster header and gets compressed before being sent to the sales gas coalescer and on to the sales gas splitter.

## Major Included Equipment

Ariel KBX/X, reciprocating compressor driven by a DOL driver with a soft-start. Compressor 8 Pole Induction Motor, rated 4000V/3PH/60Hz, 891 RPM. All cylinders dedicated.; Air Cooler, forced air intercooler after each cylinder stage, mounted on common frame, API 661. Horizontal Air-flow type, single fan. Automated louver control.; Suction Scrubber after each cylinder booster stage. Two Phase c/w Vertical flow Mesh/Vane. Vendor to design size and capacity.; Packing vent/drain separation pot - Two phase, DP: 101 kPag, Vendor to design size & capacity.; Seal Pot Waste oil transfer pump – Vendor to design size and capacity.; Filter Coalescer 0.3 microns @ 99.97%, c/w bandlock type QOC. 100 MMSCFD design flow.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Shipping compressor package to site, installation on piles, tie-in piping, electrical connections. Mounting platform and stairs. Capacity/design throughput: Design capacity is 140 MMSCFD. No turndown. Driver: Electric induction motor, 8-pole, 1000KW (1340 HP), 4000V/3PH/60Hz @ 891 rpm fixed speed. TEFC or WPII, non-sparking bidirectional cooling fan. NEMA MG 1. Operating conditions: Suction design pressure is 6137 kPag (890 psig); Discharge design pressure is 12866 kPag (1866 psig); Operating temperature at inlet is 110 F (43.3 C) summer conditions. Design conditions: Design capacity is 3962 e3m3/day (140 MMSCFD); Suction design pressure is 6137 kPag (890 psig); Discharge design pressure is 12866 kPag (1866 psig); Suction scrubber inlet liquid density assumption of 0.61 SG. |
| --- |

# 26020-01-PT-12-005 - Stabilizer Overheads Compressors

| Location / Status | 4-25 West Doe Deepcut; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-01-PT-RFQ-12-005_Stabilizer_OH_Comp.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply Stabilizer Overhead Compressor Packages consist of Two (2x) identical induction motor-driven separable reciprocating compressor packages; each designed for 100% capacity. Process function: Stabilizer Overhead Compressor compresses and recycles multiple streams from 50 psig to 1100 psig. Final discharge gas exits and is routed to the amine inlet filter coalescer or recycled back to the first stage.

## Major Included Equipment

Ariel KBC/6 four-stage separable reciprocating compressor packages.; Compressor Motor: 2700 HP with speed control and automated recycle valve, 4000V,3PH,60Hz. No Toshiba motors.; Air Cooler forced air intercooler after each stage of compression. Mounted on common frame, Ap-661 (modified). Warm air recirculation plenum heater. Non-sparking, bidirectional cooling.; 1st Stage scrubber – Two phase c/w with Cyclonic Element. Vendor to design size/capacity.; 2nd/3rd/4th stage scrubber - Two phase c/w Demister. Vendor to design Size and Capacity.; Packing vent/drain separation pot - Two phase, DP: 101 Kpag, Vendor to design size & capacity.; Vacuum Pump - Vendor to design size & capacity.; Seal Pot waste oil transfer pump - Vendor to design size and capacity. size and capacity.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Shipping compressor packages to site, installation on piles, tie-in piping, electrical connections. Mounting platform and stairs. Capacity/design throughput: Design Capacity: Stage 1 = 2.5 MMSCFD. Stage 2 = 5 MMSCFD. Stage 3 = 17 MMSCFD. Stage 4 = 17 MMSCFD. 3:1 Turndown ratio. Driver: 8 Pole electric induction motor: 2700 HP @ 891 RPM with speed control, 4000V,3PH,60Hz. Enclosure TBD (quote TEFC), non-spring bidirectional cooling fans. Tested/Labelled to NEMA standard MG 1. No Toshiba Motors. Operating conditions: Operating Pressure: 1st stage Sucton (345 kPag), discharge (799.09 kpag); 2nd stage suction (723.48 kPag), discharge (1696.74 kPag); 3rd stage suction (1594.72 kPag), discharge (3600.16 kPag); 4th stage suction (3423.59 kPag), compression (7585 kPag). Cooler discharge temperatures: 1st stage (65.56 C),2nd stage (87.78 C), 3rd. Design conditions: Design temperatures: 149 C at each stage suction, 177C at each stage discharge. MAWP is 1723 kPag at 1st stage suction. Minimum MAWP for final stage discharge is 9101 kPag at 177 C. Other MAWP is TBC. |
| --- |

# 26020-01-PT-15-001 - Process Heat Medium Unit

| Location / Status | 4-25 West Doe Deepcut; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-01-PT-15-001_Heat Medium System.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply Pump Module – One expansion tank, 3 pumps.; Medium heater Module – One medium heater, one heater blower and one air intake pre-heater. Process function: The hot oil/heat medium heating system is designed to provided heat to various process users in the facility. A thermal heating fluid is pumped in a closed loop. The system is designed with a hot loop and a cold loop so that mixing of hot and cold heat medium can be used to supply optimum.

## Major Included Equipment

Pump Module:; Expansion tank - Horizontal with Size & Capacity to be advised by vendor. Expansion volume at 274C to fill expansion tank max. 85%.; Expansion tank operating pressure 125 to 240 Kapg (18 to 23 Psig). depending on the heat medium NPSHR; Heat medium pumps - Single stage vertical inline, 3x 66%. Design pressure 2413 Kpag (350 Psig), Operating pressure 695 Kpag (100 Psig) and temp.; Assumed start up temp. 20C (68F); max. bulk temp. 260C (500F).; Heat medium system - Use Petro Canada Peterotherm product as heat medium transfer fluid.; Medium Heater Module:

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Interconnecting piping, DCS integration, foundations, mounting buildings at site, electrical supply from MCC, Platforms, stairs etc. Capacity/design throughput: -. Driver: Vendor-supplied. Operating conditions: Heat medium pumps - Operating pressure 695 Kpag (100 Psig); Expansion Tank - Operating pressure 695Kpag (100 Psig), Operating Temp. 20C to 260C. Design conditions: Heat medium pumps - Design pressure 2413 Kpag (350 Psig), Design Temp. 274C; Total Heat medium supply 151.6 M3/day (approximate). |

# 26020-01-PT-16-001 - Inlet TEG Cross Exchanger

| Location / Status | 4-25 West Doe Deepcut; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-01-PT-RFQ-16-001-Heat_Ex_ST.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply (1x) Inlet/TEG DEHY Cross Exchanger (E-5718-1: TEMA ‘R’ BEM Duty: 5514.3 kW (18.82 MMBTU/hr) and accompanying piping/instrumentation and skid. Process function: Cold Sour gas from the inlet separator flows to the TEG heat exchanger, where it exchanges heat with sweet gas leaving the amine sweetening unit. The sour gas flows to the inlet compressors.

## Major Included Equipment

(1x) Inlet/TEG DEHY Cross Exchanger (E-5718-1): TEMA ‘R’ BEM Duty: 5514.3 kW (18.82 MMBTU/hr).; TEMA Class R heat exchanger.; Bonnet front end stationary head, one pass Shell, fixed tube sheet read end head.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Interconnecting piping, DCS integration, foundations, electrical supply to MCC. Capacity/design throughput: Duty: 5514.3 kW (18.82 MMBTU/hr); Estimated 4480.28 m6/hr treated gas sour gas flowing through one side of the exchanger; Estimated 7236.64 m3/hr sour gas through the other side of the exchanger. Driver: No Driver. Operating conditions: Sour gas inlet: TBC; Sour gas outlet: 4355 kPag, 28 C; Sweet gas inlet: 7640.9 kPag 50 C; Sweet gas outlet: 7430 kPag, 16 C. Design conditions: 9756 kPag (1415 psig) design pressure; 66 C design temperature; Ambient Design Temperatures: -40 Min., +35 Max. (°C). |
| --- |

Supply one HP flare knock-out drum and one HP flare KO drum transfer pump.

# 26020-01-PT-17-004 - Inlet Separators

| Location / Status | 4-25 West Doe Deepcut; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-01-PT-RFQ-17-004_Inlet Separators 2_R1.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply Two (2) Identical Horizontal three-phase separators. Process function: The separator receives raw inlet gas from upstream and separates it into three phases: sour natural gas (vapour), sour raw condensate (light liquid), and sour water (heavy liquid).

## Major Included Equipment

Two (2) Identical Horizontal three-phase separators; Sparing: 2 x 50%; Heated self-framing building to cover instrumentation and one vessel end; Internally coated with Devchem 253 (excluding piping); Fitted with a manually adjustable weir, vertical (horizontal-flow) high-performance mesh/vane mist eliminator, and de-sanding nozzles; Two parallel Inlet PCVs: balanced globe valve with hardened trim (one for main inlet, and one for drive gas connection); Outlet manual isolation valve; Skid edge inlet isolation valve used for PCV maintenance isolation

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Interconnecting piping at skid edge, DCS (Distributed Control System) integration, foundations, electrical power supply from plant MCC (Motor Control Centre), and installation / erection. Capacity/design throughput: Slug Capacity: 33.9 m3; Water Retention Time: TBC; Condensate Retention Time: TBC; Throughput / Flowrate: 3,184 – 6,368 e3m3/d (112.5 - 225 MMSCFD). Operating conditions: Pressure: 4,502 to 4,998 kPag (653 to 725 psig); Temperature: 12.5 to 33.2 °C; Gas Flow Rate: 3,184 e3m3/d (112.5 MMSCFD); Produced Water Flow Rate: 10 Am3/d; Raw Condensate Flow Rate: 616 Am3/d. Design conditions: Pressure: 9377 kPag; Temperature: 2.8 to 34 °C; Gas Flow Rate: 6,368 e3m3/d (225 MMSCFD); Produced Water Flow Rate: 10 m3/d; Raw Condensate Flow Rate: 616 m3/d. |
| --- |

# 26020-01-PT-17-005 - Inlet Stabilizers

| Location / Status | 4-25 West Doe Deepcut; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-01-PT-RFQ-17-005_Inlet Stabilizers_R0.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply of three (3) Inlet Stabilizer Packages. Process function: The stabilization units receive raw condensate from the medium pressure flash feed separator (MPFF) bottoms and enters the stabilizer flash feed separator to separate remaining traces of produced water. The liquid hydrocarbon from the separator then passes through basket strainers and is pumped by the stabilizer feed pumps to the stabilizer feeds bottoms exchanger. It.

## Major Included Equipment

Supply of three (3) Inlet Stabilizer Packages; Design rate of 1,272 m3/d (8,000 bbl/d) for each package; 3 x 40%; Equipment Required:; Trayed Reboiled Distillation Columns; 20 floating valve trays; One (1) LIT; One (1) TIT

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Interconnecting piping at skid edge, DCS (Distributed Control System) integration, foundations, electrical power supply from plant MCC (Motor Control Centre), and installation / erection. Capacity/design throughput: Design rate of 1,272 m3/d (8,000 bbl/d) for each package; Turndown Ratio: 3:1. Driver: Feed Pumps: Electric motor; VFD compatible; Product Cooler Fan: Electric motor; VFD compatible. Operating conditions: Flash Feed Separator; Pressure: 345 kPag; Temperature: 30.6 °C; Retention Time: approximately 15 minutes; Feed / Bottoms Exchangers; Liquid hydrocarbons pre-heated to 71 °C; Stabilizer Column; Inlet Temperature: 71 °C. Design conditions: Flash Feed Separator; Design Inlet Pressure: 1724 kPag; Design Inlet Temperature: 60 °C; Feed / Bottoms Exchanger; The exchanger is to be designed for a 16.7 °C (30 °F) minimum approach; Stabilizer Column; Minimum pressure of 793 kPag; Turndown ratio: 3:1; Stabilizer Product Cooler; 130%. |
| --- |

# 26020-01-PT-17-006 - MPFF Separator

| Location / Status | 4-25 West Doe Deepcut; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-01-PT-RFQ-17-006_MPFF Separator_R0.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply One (1) Medium Pressure Flash Feed (MPFF) Separator – Two Phase. Process function: The medium pressure flash feed separator is a two-phase separator that flashes light hydrocarbon vapours from the inlet separator raw condensate. The purpose is to minimize / reduce stabilizer overheads compressor re-compression power. Flashed overhead vapours are pressure controlled to the stabilizer overheads compressor 3rd stage suction. Bottoms liquids (condensate) are level controlled.

## Major Included Equipment

One (1) Medium Pressure Flash Feed (MPFF) Separator – Two Phase; Fitted with a mistex mist extractor; Heated self-framing building module enclosing instrumentation and one vessel end; Two (2) PITs; One (1) TIT; One (1) LT for condensate level; One (1) LT for produced water level; One (1) inlet LCV

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Interconnecting piping at skid edge, DCS (Distributed Control System) integration, foundations, electrical power supply from plant MCC (Motor Control Centre), and installation / erection. Capacity/design throughput: Normal Throughput: 7,883 m3/h; Maximum Throughput: 15,223 m3/h. Operating conditions: Pressure: 1,724 kPag; Inlet Flow rate: 7,883 m3/h; Temperature: -9.55 °C. Design conditions: Design Pressure: TBD; Flange Rating: ASME Class 300; Inlet Flow Rate: 15,223 m3/h; Design Temperature: -40 °C. |
| --- |

# 26020-01-PT-17-007 - Pressure Vessels (Storage Bullets)

| Location / Status | 3-25 liquids area; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-01-PT-RFQ-17-007-_Storage_Bullets.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply two unstable condensate storage bullets and sixteen LPG product storage bullets for C5 condensate and LPG product storage.

## Major Included Equipment

Two unstable condensate storage bullets and sixteen LPG product storage bullets. Each bullet is 3658 mm ID x 42494 mm S/S, nominally 454 m3 / 120,000 US gal at 84% maximum volume, designed for 1724 kPag at 66 C and full vacuum, with outdoor saddle mounting and stairs/platforms.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: Unstable condensate bullets store condensate from the C5 product line before the condensate transfer pumps. LPG bullets store C3, C4, or C3/C4 LPG mix from the depropanizer before transfer to the off-site liquids hub through booster pumps. LPG vapour equalization must avoid pockets; butane storage requires blanket gas. Foundations, DCS integration, and electrical supply to MCC are by others. |
| --- |

# 26020-01-PT-18-002 - Tank Pumps

| Location / Status | 4-25 West Doe Deepcut; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-01-PT-RFQ-18-002-Tank_Farm_Pump.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply 2 Identical Water Transfer Pumps/Motors; 2 Identical Condensate Transfer Pumps/Motors; 1 Condensate Recycle Pump/Motor with strainer; 2 Identical Pumps for Process water Transfer. Process function: (2x) Water Transfer Pump: pull water from the produced water tanks and push it to the 3-25 compressor station.; (2x) Condensate Transfer Pump: Transfer condensate product to downstream liquids hub. Designed to have 350 kPad (50 psid) differential to travel to hub.; (1x) Condensate recycle pump: Recycles condensate from the produced water tank condensate skim.

## Major Included Equipment

(2x) Water Transfer Pump (P-9290-1/P9293-1). Radial Centrifugal pump. Seal Plan: Single Mechanical. 218 m3/d @ TBC TDH - 7.64 KW of power. Designed.; (2x) Condensate Transfer Pump(P-9210-1/P9220-1). Axial Flow Multistage Horizontal Centrifugal Pump. Seal Plan: API-682, 14/52. 24 m3/h @ TBC TDH. 2x @ 150%. System.; (1x) Condensate Recycle Pump (P-9200-1). Diaphragm Positive Displacement Hydrocell. Sealless c/w discharge pulsation dampener. 20m3/h @ TBC TDH. Strainer to be placed before inlet.; (2x) 100% process water transfer radial centrifugal pumps. (Vendor to design); (2x) 100% fresh caustic transfer radial centrifugal pumps. Flow control and density to maintain caustic solution (Vendor to design). Corrosive Service. No Aluminium.; Self-framing building to be erected at site.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: DCS integration, foundations, electrical supply to MCC. Capacity/design throughput: Design Capacities:; Water Pump: 218 m3/d @ TBC TDH. Each sized to 100% capacity; Condensate Transfer Pump: 24 m3/h @ TBC TDH. Each sized to 150% facility design flow of combined condensate product.; Condensate Recycle Pump: 20 m3/h @ TBC TDH. Sized to 100% capacity. Driver: All pumps driven by 575V/3PH/60Hz motors. Starting method to be DOL or VFD. To include local control (H-O-A or On-Off switch. Electric motor fed from 600V MCC. Motors to be sized for inlet stabilizer composition density at -40C start-up condition. Operating conditions: Operating Specs TBC. See Throughput/Capacity and site conditions. Design conditions: Operating Specs TBC. See Throughput/Capacity and site conditions. |
| --- |

| Location / Status | 4-25 West Doe Deepcut; budgetary RFQ basis; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-01-PT-RFQ-18-003-LPG_Loading_Pumps.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply 4 Identical Blackmer Model LGL4B Rotary Vane Pumps set up in parallel. Process function: Pumps to move LPG product from storage to LPG Truck Loading.

## Major Included Equipment

(4x) LPG Loading Pumps (P9510/20/30/40-1). Blackmer Model LGL4B Rotary Vane Pumps. Capacity: 68 m3/hr @ 345 kPad (300 USGPM @ 50 psid) TBC TDH.; Self-framing building to be erected at site.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: DCS integration, foundations, electrical supply to MCC. Capacity/design throughput: Capacity per Pump: 68 m3/hr @ 345 kPad (300 USGPM @ 50 psid) TBC TDH. Driver: Electric 575V/3PH/60Hz motors. To include local control (H-O-A or On-Off switch. Electric motor fed from 600V MCC. Motors to be sized for inlet stabilizer composition density at -40C start-up condition. Operating conditions: Operating Conditions TBC. Please see capacity/site conditions. Design conditions: Operating Conditions TBC. Please see capacity/site conditions. |

# 26020-01-PT-18-004 - LPG Booster

| Location / Status | 4-25 West Doe Deepcut; budgetary RFQ basis; project-specific budgetary RFQ scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-01-PT-18-004-LPG-Booster.pdf |

## Basic Scope

Supply two LPG booster pumps arranged in parallel for transfer from LPG storage to the LACT unit where booster pressure may be required.

## Major Included Equipment

Two LPG booster pumps P-9570-1/P-9580-1, vertical multistage can-type, API 610 seal plan 13/52, 575 V / 3 phase / 60 Hz motors, structural skid, package piping, instrumentation, electrical, HVAC/enclosure, CRN/TSBC as applicable, and commissioning support.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: Each pump is sized for 145 m3/h at TBD TDH and 150% capacity; booster pump design differential is 25 psid / 172 kPad. DCS integration, foundations, and electrical supply to MCC are by others. |

## Basic Scope

Supply one (1) atmospheric disulphide oil (DSO) storage tank. Process function: Tank receives separated DSO from the DSO separator (level-controlled) within the caustic regeneration system.

## Major Included Equipment

Item No. DSO Storage Tank:; Design & fabrication to modified API 650,; 400 bbl, nominal capacity; Atmospheric pressure tank.; Design pressure: 32 Oz, 1.0 Oz Vacuum.; c/w heater at 32.2 °C (90 °F) minimum. Vendor to design heater.; Internally coated (floor, walls, roof); Insulated to maintain DSO above pour point for truck-out and handling. Minimum temperature TBD.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Foundations, mounting tanks at site, electrical/instrumentation, platforms, staircase etc. Capacity/design throughput: TBC. Operating conditions: -. Design conditions: Atmospheric; Minimum ambient temperature (TBD); Design pressure: 32 Oz, 1.0 Oz Vacuum.; Flow rate: TBD; 400bbl. |

## Basic Scope

Item No. 1: Two (2) 2,000 bbl Process Water Storage Tank. Process function: Sweet Produced Water & Process Water.

## Major Included Equipment

Item No. 1: Two (2) 2,000 bbl Process Water Storage Tank; Design & fabrication to modified API 650; Atmospheric storage tanks; Design pressure: 32 oz, 1.0 oz vacuum; External insulation required, heater required to prevent freezing; LP fuel gas blanket to maintain above atmospheric pressure; Non sour clean treated water; Externally insulated and heated to prevent freezing in arctic/sub-arctic conditions.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Foundations, mounting tanks at site, electrical/instrumentation, platforms, staircase etc. Capacity/design throughput: Design Flow for Item No. 1; To Amine Surge Tank: 0.55 m3/h continuous (TBD); Other throughputs: TBD. Operating conditions: Pressure: Atmospheric; Temperature: TBD for Item No. 1. Design conditions: Pressure:32 oz, 1.0 oz vacuum; Temperature: -40 °C (min) & 60 °C (max). |

# 26020-01-PT-19-003 - Tanks, Caustic

| Location / Status | 4-25 future LPG mercaptan treating unit; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-01-PT-RFQ-19-003_Tanks_Caust_2_R0.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply One (1) spent caustic tank, and one (1) fresh caustic tank. Process function: Fresh Caustic Tank: To store and supply fresh caustic solution to the caustic treatment unit.; Spent Caustic Tank: To receive and safely store spent caustic from the pressurized caustic drain drum.

## Major Included Equipment

Item No. 1, Spent Caustic Storage Tank: (TK-6780-1); Receives spent caustic from the pressurized caustic drain drum via level control; Quantity: 1; Design & fabrication to modified API 650,; 400 bbl, nominal capacity; Design pressure: 32 Oz, 1.0 Oz Vacuum.; Atmospheric pressure tank.; c/w heater at 32.2 °C (90 °F) minimum. Vendor to design the heater.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Foundations, mounting tanks at site, electrical/instrumentation, platforms, staircase etc. Capacity/design throughput: TBC. Operating conditions: -. Design conditions: Item 1; Atmospheric; Minimum ambient temperature; Flow rate: TBD; 400bbl; Item 2; Atmospheric; Minimum ambient temperature; Flow rate: TBD; 400bbl. |

# 26020-01-PT-19-004 - Tanks, Condensate

| Location / Status | 4-25 West Doe Deepcut tank farm; analog scope basis pending package RFQ or brief. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Analog basis from 26020-03-PT-19-006_Tanks_Cond/Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-03-PT-RFQ-19-006 - Conde Tanks.docx; no direct package-folder brief.md, DOCX, or PDF scope source found for 4-25. |

## Basic Scope

Supply condensate product storage tanks for 4-25 on-spec C5+ condensate product storage, using the 3-25 condensate tank package as the nearest analog.

## Major Included Equipment

Likely API 650 modified atmospheric condensate product storage tanks with blanket gas, PVRV/EPRV protection, VRU header connection, grounding/cathodic protection provisions, tank instrumentation, and standard tank appurtenances. Final 4-25 tank count, tags, capacity, and dimensions require confirmation; the Deepcut package roster indicates five condensate tanks, while the 3-25 analog uses four 3,800 bbl tanks.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: Analog 3-25 condensate service is on-spec C5+ condensate product storage downstream of treating or bypass paths before condensate export. For 4-25, confirm the tank count, tags, capacity, inlet/outlet sources, whether mercaptan treating bypass/recycle paths apply, VRU/blanket gas requirements, and whether design conditions match the 3-25 3,800 bbl tank basis. |

Two API 650 modified atmospheric produced-water storage tanks, expected tags TK-9010-1 and TK-9020-1, with internal coating, external insulation and electric heating where required, PVRV/EPRV protection, LP fuel gas blanket, VRU suction/header connection as applicable, tank instrumentation, and standard tank appurtenances.

# 26020-01-PT-22-003 - Mole Sieve Drier Unit (NGL)

| Location / Status | 4-25 NGL service; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-01-PT-RFQ-22-003_NGL-Mole-Sieve-Dehy.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply One (1) NGL molecular sieve dehydration package, three-tower configuration (one tower in adsorption, one in regeneration, one on standby), rated 2,385 m³/d (15,000 bbl/d), processing water-saturated C3+ NGL to a target outlet water content of less than 7 ppmw.

## Major Included Equipment

Inlet NGL pretreatment and free-water removal upstream of the molecular sieve beds, including liquid/liquid coalescer(s) with coalesced water level control to the produced water.; Three (3) molecular sieve dehydration vessels with 3A molecular sieve adsorbent and silica gel layer for liquid carryover protection, complete with internals, valves, drains.; Regeneration-gas heating, cooling (aerial cooler with automated warm-air recirculation louvers, automated intake louvers, and plenum heating bundle for winterization; split-header design), three-phase scrubber with mist pad, and pressure relief provisions.; Outlet particulate filtration on dry NGL leaving the molecular sieve dehydrator.; Local package instrumentation and analyzer interface for one (1) moisture analyzer with vaporizing regulator for C3+ sampling system.; Heated building/enclosure housing inlet gas coalescer, inline mixers, settling vessel, and regeneration gas scrubber, as required by final heater/scrubber location and area classification.; Package utility, electrical, flare, drain, and control-system tie-in provisions to package battery limits.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Upstream NGL mercaptan treating process package and caustic process-provider design.; Downstream NGL storage bullets, NGL loading, LACT, and product export systems.; Downstream Sales gas compressors and stabilizer overheads compressor package.; Produced-water tank, produced-water drain header, and facility drain infrastructure beyond package nozzles/tie-ins.; Flare header. Operating conditions: Adsorption side (per latest DBM SEC-07):; Inlet pressure: 1,978 kPag design (low/high TBC).; Outlet pressure: 1,943 kPag design (low/high TBC).; Inlet temperature: 29.7 °C (low) / 46.3 °C (design) / 51.8 °C (high).; Inlet water content: saturated at design inlet conditions and flow.; Regeneration side. Design conditions: Adsorption bed pressure drop:; Start of life: less than 4 psid / 27.6 kPad.; End of life: less than 10 psid (including vessel nozzles).; Cycle basis (preliminary):; Adsorption: 24 hours.; Draining, heating ramp, regeneration pre-heat/hold, heating, cooling, filling, standby, and total regeneration cycle: TBC by. |
| --- |

# 26020-01-PT-23-001 - Fuel Gas Skid

| Location / Status | 4-25 West Doe Deepcut; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-01-PT-RFQ-23-001_FG_Skid_2.docx |

## Basic Scope

Package will include 1 skid mounted Low Pressure Fuel Gas Package comprising of a Low-pressure fuel gas heater and 1 low pressure fuel gas scrubber. Process function: Skid will serve the low-pressure fuel gas system for the West Doe Deep Cut Facility.

## Major Included Equipment

A skid for the system to be mounted on.; A fuel gas heater capacity TBD. Heater to be controlled by SCR (600V). The heater shall include skin temperature thermocouple override control on heater.; A fuel gas scrubber shall be sized using a k factor of 0.35 (imperial) maximum plus de-ration factor for operating pressure. Vendor to design.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Shipping packages to site, installation, tie-in piping, electrical tie-in etc. Capacity/design throughput: Design Flow Required > 8.4 MMSCFD (237.5 e3m3/day). Gas heated to 95 F (35 C) Final Flow TBD. Driver: SCR heater control panels are 600V, located in electrical building. Operating conditions: Operating Pressure is 150 psig, Ambient Temperature is -19 C to 22.2 C. Design conditions: Design Pressure is 150 psig. Design Temperature is -40 C to 35 C. MAWP is TBD. |
| --- |

# 26020-01-PT-27-001 - Amine Treating Unit

| Location / Status | 4-25 West Doe Deepcut; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-01-PT-RFQ-27-001_Amine_Treat_Unit_R0.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply The vendor scope of supply comprises a complete Amine Treating Unit (ATU). Process function: The ATU removes H₂S and CO₂ from sour natural gas using a continuous MDEA absorption-regeneration cycle, operating across two modules.; Module 1 — Amine Gas Sweetening: Conditions sour inlet gas and contacts it with lean MDEA solvent to produce pipeline-quality sweet gas.; Module 2 — Amine Regeneration: Thermally regenerates the rich amine, returning lean solvent.

## Major Included Equipment

Amine Treating Unit will be as follows:; Amine Gas Sweetening Module; Amine Inlet Filter Coalescer; Quantity: 2 x 100% (piping configured for on filter is always operational); 0.3 Micron @ 99.97%; Single phase; c/w Bandlock Type QOC; Pressure Drop < 2 psid clean

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Shipping packages to site, installation on piles, tie-in piping, electrical connections. Mounting platform and stairs etc.; Process water pumps and tanks done separate from this package. Capacity/design throughput: See Appendix A. Driver: All pumps require electric motors. Motors rated 1 HP to 200 HP shall be 600V / 3-phase / 60 Hz. Motors rated above 200 HP shall be 4160V or 6900V / 3-phase / 60 Hz. All motors 100 HP and above shall be VFD-ready. Operating conditions: -. Design conditions: See Appendix A. |
| --- |

# 26020-01-PT-27-002 - Caustic Treating (NGL Mercaptan Removal)

| Location / Status | 4-25 West Doe Deepcut NGL mercaptan treating; package brief scope basis; PDF pricing/delivery go-by. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/brief.md; budgetary pricing/delivery go-by only: Bid Docs/Budgetary/7304 Millenia Proposal Rev. 0.pdf |

## Basic Scope

Supply a complete caustic treating system for NGL/C3+ mercaptan removal immediately downstream of the deethanizer.

## Major Included Equipment

Caustic contactor/mixer equipment, caustic outlet filtration, water wash and coalescing equipment, caustic regeneration equipment, heaters/exchangers, circulation and transfer pumps, pressurized caustic drain drum, DSO handling, caustic storage interfaces, incinerator interface, instrumentation, controls, and building-contained caustic equipment.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: The brief flags a conflict between a regenerative DBM basis and a non-regenerative SCA-001/VE-8 basis. Vendor and process provider must confirm the governing process basis. Thirteen tagged items are listed, with one duplicated tag requiring reconciliation. |
| --- |

# 26020-01-PT-28-001 - Cryogenic Deep Cut Package

| Location / Status | 4-25 West Doe Deepcut; fixed-price cost estimate / EPF scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/300MMScfd Deep Cut EPF Scope of Supply.docx |

## Basic Scope

Provide package engineering, procurement, and fabrication for a complete cryogenic deep-cut propane-plus recovery package to dehydrate and recover C3+ hydrocarbons from a pressurized gas stream.

## Major Included Equipment

Inlet filter coalescers, molecular sieve dehydration system, BAHX cold box, turboexpander/recompression system, deethanizer tower, methanol injection, regeneration gas equipment, dust filters, regeneration gas compressors, regeneration gas cooler/separator/recycle systems, analyzers, and associated auxiliaries.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: Design basis is approximately 305 MMSCFD inlet gas. Process scope runs from gas downstream of upstream compression/amine/TEG dehydration to turboexpander recompressor discharge upstream of sales compression. Main process pipe rack and interconnect piping from skid edge are outside the package scope. |
| --- |

# 26020-01-PT-29-001 - Lube Oil Supply

| Location / Status | 4-25 West Doe Deepcut; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-01-PT-RFQ-29-001-Lube oil supply.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply of two (2) lube oil transfer pumps for the West Doe Deepcut facility Storage Tank Area, serving all compressor packages on site. Process function: Lub oil supply.

## Major Included Equipment

Supply of two (2) lube oil transfer pumps for the West Doe Deepcut facility Storage Tank Area, serving all compressor packages on site; P-9240-1: Cylinder Lube Oil Transfer Pump – fills frame day tanks of various compressor packages with cylinder lubricating oil from 400 bbl heated Compressor.; P-9250-1: Crankcase Lube Oil Transfer Pump – fills frame day tanks of various compressor packages with crankcase lube oil from 200 bbl heated Compressor.; Horizontal storage tank, split in the middle to store both lube oils (crank case and compressor packing).; Pumps are electric motor driven; Sweet and sour service.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Shipping the package to site, installation on piles, tie-in piping, electrical connections. Mounting platform and stairs. Driver: No Toshiba motors. Operating conditions: Low / atmospheric (lube oil transfer pump service); Ambient to heated tank temperature. Design conditions: Low / atmospheric (lube oil transfer pump service); Ambient to heated tank temperature. |

# 26020-01-PT-35-001 - Pig Receivers (Inlet)

| Location / Status | 4-25 West Doe Deepcut inlet pipeline; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-01-PT-RFQ-35-001-Pig_Recv_2.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply 3 identical 610mm (24”) OD Pig receivers with HIPPS package on dedicated structural steel non-enclosed skids. Process function: Plant inlet pipeline gas will enter the facility through the pig receivers installed and move on into the inlet separators.

## Major Included Equipment

(3x) Pig receiver assemblies: (PR-1010/1020/1030-1) 610mm O.D (24”) pig receiver installed on a dedicated structural steel non-enclosed skid. All skid mounted isolation or ESDV.; Each receiver skid to include a High Integrity Pressure Protection System (HIPPS) package with ESDV’s upstream of the pig receiver.; HIPPS package to have a pressure control valve and outlet pressure transmitter, via PID control to maintain inlet separator vessel operating pressure below applicable.; HIPPS package Must include shutdown valve using pneumatic hi-low shutoff, with an additional shutdown valve and pneumatic hi-low for redundancy, to close high-pressure inlet.; Sour service: Design is 1.0 mol%; Sweet gas purge downstream of manual isolation valve for sour gas purge or receiver barrel prior to opening for pig retrieval.; Vent line to HP flare system.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: interconnecting piping, DCS integration, foundations, electrical supply to MCC. Capacity/design throughput: design throughput = 225 MMSCFD. Operating conditions: Historical Ambient Temperature: -19 Min., 22.2 Max. (°C),; Normal Operating Pressure: 653 psig to 725 psig, MAOP = 1300 psig; Normal flowrate per receiver TBC. Design conditions: Design pressure: low = 653 psig, normal high = 725 psig, MAWP = 1440 psig; Ambient Design Temperature: -40 Min., +35 Max. (°C). |
| --- |

# 26020-01-PT-39-001 - Instrument Air Building

| Location / Status | Shared 4-25 and 3-25 facility service; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-01-PT-RFQ-39-001_Instr_Air_Bldg_R1.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply Instrument air package consisting of two motor-driven, oil-injected rotary screw air compressors, one wet air receiver, two dryer pre-filters, one regenerative desiccant air dryers, one after-filter, and one dry air receiver (or two 50% capacity receivers).

## Major Included Equipment

Instrument air package shall be designed such that all PSVs in the system are set at 948 kPag (137.5 psig); Delivered instrument air shall have a maximum water dew point of -73.3 °C at 1000 kPag.; 2 x oil-injected, rotary screw, air-cooled instrument air compressors, each rated 1113 SCFM at 861 kPag (125 psig) discharge pressure, driven by electric motors.; 2 x 250 HP electric motors, complete with soft starter or VFD ready, and anti-condensation space heaters.; 1 x wet air receiver, sized by vendor.; 2 x dryer pre-filters, sized by vendor.; 1x Air dryers 100% capacity (2 tanks/towers, one operating, one regenerating), size and capacity TBD by vendor. Dryer sized for 2 compressors and leave.; 1 x common after-filter located downstream of the combined dryer outlets.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Shipping compressor packages to site, installation on piles, tie-in piping, electrical connections. Mounting platform and stairs. Capacity/design throughput: 1113 SCFM at 861 kPag (125 psig). Driver: Electric motors with soft starter or VFD ready, 200-250 HP 600V/3PH/60HZ. Speed (to be determined by the vendor), TEFC, Non-classified. Operating conditions: Instrument air compressor max discharge pressure/shutdown is 1000 kPag; Maximum system design pressure 1034 kPag (150 psig); Minimum system pressure 551 kPag (80 psig); Facility shutdown pressure 482 kPag (70 psig); Design temperature -40C to 38C; Maximum water dewpoint of -73.3 °C @ 1000 kPag. Design conditions: Design temperature -40C to 38C,; System design max pressure 1034 kPag (150 psig). |
| --- |

# 26020-02-PT-12-001 - Inlet Compressors

| Location / Status | 3-25 West Doe Compressor Station; package brief scope basis; PDF pricing/delivery go-by. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/brief.md; budgetary pricing/delivery go-by only: Bid Docs/Budgetary/24292-02-PT-ENR-12-201_Compressors_R2.pdf |

## Basic Scope

Supply two identical parallel sour inlet gas reciprocating compressor packages.

## Major Included Equipment

Two Ariel KBZ/6 separable reciprocating compressor packages, two-stage compression with intercooling and aftercooling, modular self-framing buildings, piping, instrumentation, electrical, HVAC, and package auxiliaries.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: Configuration is 2 x 50% with no dedicated spare. Each unit is 40 MMSCFD for 80 MMSCFD combined, compressing approximately 1275 kPag suction to 6550 kPag discharge. NACE-compliant materials and seals are required. |
| --- |

Supply two HP flare KO drums and two dedicated transfer pumps.

## Major Included Equipment

HP flare KO drums V-4100-2 and V-4150-2, transfer pumps P-4100-2 and P-4150-2, liquid transfer to condensate slop tank, truck-out provision, and package interfaces.

# 26020-02-PT-17-003 - Inlet Separators

| Location / Status | 3-25 West Doe Compressor Station; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-02-PT-RFQ-17-003_Inlet Separators 1_R0.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply Two (2) Identical Horizontal three-phase separators. Process function: The separator receives raw inlet gas from upstream pipeline pig receiver and separates it into three phases: sour natural gas (vapour), sour raw condensate (light liquid), and sour water (heavy liquid).

## Major Included Equipment

Two (2) Identical Horizontal three-phase separators; Sparing: 2 x 50%; Heated self-framing building to cover instrumentation and one vessel end; Internally coated with Devchem 253 (excluding piping); Fitted with a manually adjustable weir, vertical (horizontal-flow) high-performance mesh/vane mist eliminator, and de-sanding nozzles; Inlet PCV: balanced globe valve with hardened trim; Outlet manual isolation valve; Two (2) produced water LCVs to be installed in parallel

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Interconnecting piping at skid edge, DCS (Distributed Control System) integration, foundations, electrical power supply from plant MCC (Motor Control Centre), and installation / erection. Capacity/design throughput: Slug Capacity: 31.8 m3; Water Retention Time: 4.35 to 8.7 minutes; Condensate Retention Time: 55 to 110 minutes; Throughput / Flowrate: 1,132 - 2,264 e3m3/d (40 – 80 MMSCFD). Operating conditions: Pressure: 862 to 3944 kPag (125 to 572 psig); Temperature: 4 to 15 °C; Gas Flow Rate: 1,132 e3m3/d (80 MMSCFD); Produced Water Flow Rate: 1,800 Am3/d; Raw Condensate Flow Rate: 556 Am3/d. Design conditions: Pressure: 4973 kPag; Temperature: 4 to 15 °C; Gas Flow Rate: 2,264 e3m3/d (80 MMSCFD); Produced Water Flow Rate: 3,600 m3/d; Raw Condensate Flow Rate: 1,111 m3/d. |
| --- |

# 26020-02-PT-23-001 - Fuel Gas Skid

| Location / Status | 3-25 West Doe Compressor Station; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-02-PT-RFQ-23-001_FG_Skid_1.docx |

## Basic Scope

Package will include 1 skid mounted Low Pressure Fuel Gas Package comprising of a Low-pressure fuel gas heater and 1 low pressure fuel gas scrubber. Process function: Skid will serve the low-pressure fuel gas system for the West Doe Deep Cut Facility.

## Major Included Equipment

A skid for the system to be mounted on.; A fuel gas heater capacity TBD. Heater to be controlled by SCR (600V). The heater shall include skin temperature thermocouple override control on heater.; A fuel gas scrubber shall be sized using a k factor of 0.35 (imperial) maximum plus de-ration factor for operating pressure. Vendor to design.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Shipping packages to site, installation, tie-in piping, electrical tie-in. Capacity/design throughput: Design Flow Required > 1.5 MMSCFD (42.5 e3m3/day). Gas heated to 95 F (35 C) Final Flow TBD. Driver: SCR heater control panels are 600V, located in electrical building. Operating conditions: Operating Pressure is 150 psig, Ambient Temperature is -19 C to 22.2 C. Design conditions: Design Pressure is 150 psig. Design Temperature is -40 C to 35 C. MAWP is TBD. Heating Value is 1040 BTU/SCF. |
| --- |

# 26020-02-PT-25-003 - Incinerator

| Location / Status | Physically near 3-25 flare stacks; services 4-25 NGL mercaptan treating; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-01-PT-RFQ-25-003_Incinerator.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply Qty 1 incinerator knockout drum; Qty 1 incinerator knockout drum transfer pump; Qty 1 incinerator – low pressure flare stack; Qty 1 incinerator blower. Process function: Vapours from the spent caustic storage tank and the caustic regeneration column overheads flow to the incinerator.

## Major Included Equipment

QTY 1: Incinerator Knock Out Drum (V-6900-1): 2,896 mm (114”) ID x 9,144 mm (30’) S/S. 345 kpag @ -45.5C/260C, 1.59 mm C.A (50.; QTY 1: Incinerator KO Drum Transfer Pump (P-6900-1): Vertical Inline Centrifugal Pump. 70 USGPM flow rate. 5 hp, 575V/3Ph/60Hz motor. Class 1 Div 2; QTY 1: Incinerator (FL-6920-1): NPS 6 x 54861 mm (180’) tall. Capable of handling max relief rate from tank farm and VRU with less.; QTY 1: Incinerator Blower (B-6920-1): ARR-4 Disch. Vun. TEFC, 405T Fram Furnace. 74 kW (100hp), 575V/3Ph/60hz 1800 rpm motor. Type VJ, CLII.; See PFD for instrumentation; Self-framing building to be erected at site.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: DCS integration, foundations, electrical supply to MCC. Capacity/design throughput: Preliminary design flow is 0.6 MMSCFD (17 E3M3D) for the incinerator and Knockout Drum; Design throughput for the Incinerator KO Drum Transfer Pump is 70 USGPM. Driver: Transfer Pump: 5 hp, 575V/3Ph/60Hz motor.; Blower Motor: 74 kW (100hp), 575V/3Ph/60hz 1800 rpm motor. Type VJ, CLII.; VFD or soft start required for motors >= 100 HP. Operating conditions: See Appendix A for Fluid Operating Conditions, Pressures, Temperatures; See attached PFD for design/layout. Design conditions: Design Ambient Temp. -40 Min., +35 Max. (°C); Knockout Drum: Design Pressure = 345 kPag/50 psig; Incinerator Operating pressure = 1 psig (7k Pag). |
| --- |

# 26020-02-PT-27-001 - Caustic Treating (Condensate Mercaptan Removal)

| Location / Status | 3-25 West Doe Liquids Hub; package brief scope basis; PDF pricing/delivery go-by. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/brief.md; budgetary pricing/delivery go-by only: Bid Docs/Budgetary/7304 Millenia Proposal Rev. 0.pdf |

## Basic Scope

Supply a non-regenerable caustic treating package for C5+ condensate mercaptan removal.

## Major Included Equipment

Caustic contactor/treating equipment, pumps, indoor caustic-area equipment, instrumentation, piping, controls, and related package systems for 20,000 BPD C5+ condensate treating.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: Package target is treated product with C1-C3 RSH below 175 ppmw S and total sulphur below 0.5 wt%, with a possible volatile mercaptan waiver requiring confirmation. All caustic treating equipment is indoor; no aluminum is permitted in the caustic building. |
| --- |

# 26020-02-PT-35-001 - Pig Receivers (Inlet)

| Location / Status | 3-25 West Doe Compressor Station; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/26020-02-PT-RFQ-35-001-Pig_Recv_1.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply 2 identical 610mm (24”) OD Pig receivers with ESDV on dedicated structural steel non-enclosed skids. Process function: Plant inlet pipeline gas will enter the facility through the pig receivers installed and move on into the inlet separators.

## Major Included Equipment

(2x) Pig receiver assemblies: (PR-1010/1020-2) 610mm O.D (24”) pig receiver installed on a dedicated structural steel non-enclosed skid. All skid mounted isolation or ESDV.; ESDV installed upstream of the receiver.; Sour service: Design is 0.1 mol%; Sweet gas purge downstream of manual isolation valve for sour gas purge or receiver barrel prior to opening for pig retrieval.; Vent line to HP flare system.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: interconnecting piping, DCS integration, foundations, electrical supply to MCC. Capacity/design throughput: design throughput = 80 MMSCFD. Operating conditions: Historical Ambient Temperature: -19 Min., 22.2 Max. (°C),; Normal Operating Pressure: 125 psig to 200 psig, MAOP = 572 psig; Normal flowrate per receiver TBC. Design conditions: Design pressure: low = 125 psig, normal high = 200 psig, MAWP = 635 psig; Ambient Design Temperature: -40 Min., +35 Max. (°C). |

# 26020-03-PT-12-001 - Vapour Recovery Unit

| Location / Status | 3-25 West Doe Liquids Hub; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-03-PT-RFQ-12-001_VRU_1_R0.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply two (2) complete 100% capacity VRU compressor packages in a lead-lag configuration for sour service, both housed in one building. Each train includes a two-stage Ro-Flo 12S/212M positive displacement rotary vane compressor driven by a 200 HP VFD motor.

## Major Included Equipment

Compressors (1st & 2nd Stage); Quantity: 4 total (1st and 2nd stage for Train 1; 1st and 2nd stage for Train 2). 2 x 100% capacity.; Type: Ro-Flo 12S/212M two-stage rotary vane, positive displacement.; Service: Sour service (0.4588 mol% H₂S, 0.4314 mol% CO2), NACE designation applies.; Seals: Dual mechanical pressurized barrier seal (Plan 53 type) using fuel gas, with alarms and primary vent to LP flare.; Motors; Quantity: 2 total (1 per train). Stage 1 and 2 of each train will be driven by the same motor

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Shipping compressor packages to site, installation on piles, tie-in piping, electrical connections. Mounting platform and stairs etc. Capacity/design throughput: Expected flow per unit 0.510 MMSCFD. Driver: 200 HP/ 600V / 3 phase/ 60 Hz for Compressors, VFD ready.; All cooler motors also VFD ready and 600V. Operating conditions: 1st stage intercooler outlet Temp (operating) 48.9 C; 2nd stage aftercooler outlet Temp (operating) 60.0 C; 1st stage dewpoint Temp 45.6 C; 2nd stage dewpoint Temp 53.2 C. Design conditions: 0.9 kPag (2 oz/in2) design suction pressure; 483 kPag (70 psig) design discharge pressure; 28 e3m3/d (1 MMSCFD) design capacity; Design Temp 102 C. |
| --- |

# 26020-03-PT-18-002 - Tank Farm Pumps

| Location / Status | 3-25 West Doe Liquids Hub tank farm; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-03-PT-RFQ-18-002-Tank Farm Pumps.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply 1 Building Drain Pump, pneumatic diaphragm.; 2 Water transfer pumps, radial centrifugal; 2 Sour condensate pumps, vertical inline centrifugal.; 2 Condensate Sweetening feed pumps, vertical inline centrifugal. Process function: Package houses all pumps for the tank farm. It includes the following:; Water transfer pumps set up in parallel pulling water from produced water tanks through a bag filter, and out to the produced water pipeline.; Sour Condensate Pumps move sour condensate from the sour condensate storage tanks to the condensate sweetening feed pumps.; Condensate.

## Major Included Equipment

1 Building Drain Pump (P-9295-2): Graco 1050A Pneumatic Diaphragm. 11.34m3/h @ 689 kPag. Pneumatically Driven.; (2x) Water Transfer Pump (P-9290/9293-2): Radial centrifugal, seal plan: single mechanical. Power: 150 KW (200HP). Powered by 575V/3Ph/60Hz. Water transfer line to include c/w.; (2x) Sour Condensate booster pumps (p-9215/9216-2): Vertical Inline Centrifugal Pumps. Seal Plan: API-682 Plan 14/52. Power: 18.5 k@ (25 hp). Powered by 575V/3Ph/60Hz.; (2x) Condensate Sweetening feed pumps (P-9210/9220-2): Vertical inline centrifugal pumps. Seal Plan: API-682 Plan 14/52. Power: 55 kW (75 hp) @ 100%. Powered by.; (1x) Condensate Skim Pump (P-9200-2). Diaphragm positive displacement. Hydracell, Sealess. Power: 11 kW (15 hp). Powered by 575V/3Ph/60Hz.; (1x) Sour Condensate Recycle Pump (P-9230-2). Diaphragm positive displacement. Hydracell, Sealess. Power: 11 kW (15 hp). Powered by 575V/3Ph/60Hz.; (2x) Condensate Booster Pumps (P-9211/9221-2). Vertical inline centrifugal. Aeal Plan: API-682 Plan 14/52. Power 18.5 kW (25 hp).; 1 Condensate Product recycle pump (P-9240-2): Diaphragm positive displacement. Hydracell, Sealess. Power: 11 kW (15 hp) with inlet basket strainer (size TBC).

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: DCS integration, foundations, electrical supply to MCC. Capacity/design throughput: Building Drain Pump: 11.34m3/h @ 689 kPag.; Water Transfer Pump: 218 m3/day @ 172 kPad (40 USGOM @ 25 psid); Sour Condensate Booster Pump: TBC; Sweet Condensate Feed Pump: TBC; Condensate Skim Pump: 20m3/hr; Sour Condensate Recycle Pump: 20m3/hr; Condensate Booster Pump: TBC; Condensate Loading. Driver: All pumps driven by 575V/3PH/60Hz motors. Starting method to be DOL or VFD. To include local control (H-O-A or On-Off switch. Electric motor fed from 600V MCC. Motors to be sized for inlet stabilizer composition density at -40C start-up condition. Operating conditions: Operating Specs TBC. See Throughput/Capacity and site conditions. Design conditions: Operating Specs TBC. See Throughput/Capacity and site conditions. |

## Basic Scope

Item No. 1: Two (2) 3800 bbl Sweet Produced Water Storage Tank (TK-9060-2, TK-9070-2). Process function: Sweet Produced Water & Process Water.

## Major Included Equipment

Item No. 1: Two (2) 3800 bbl Sweet Produced Water Storage Tank (TK-9060-2, TK-9070-2); Design & fabrication to modified API 650, volume: TBD; External insulation required, heater required to prevent freezing; Blanket gas (LP fuel gas) provided for vacuum prevention in winter per API-2000; Non sour produced water fluid; Internal coating on floors, walls, roofs (Devchem 253); Kennilworth-type HCL float skim system, one per tank; float design SG less than or equal to 0.67

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Foundations, mounting tanks at site, electrical/instrumentation, platforms, staircase etc. Capacity/design throughput: Design Flow for Item No. 1; 15,300 kg/h / 3584 Am3/d from comp station; 240 Am3/d from cryo; Design Flow: TBD for Item No. 2. Operating conditions: Pressure: Atmospheric; Temperature: 5 °C for Item No. 1; Temperature: TBD for Item No. 2. Design conditions: Pressure:32 oz test pressure; Temperature: -40 °C (min) & 60 °C (max). |

# 26020-03-PT-19-004 - Tanks, Slop

| Location / Status | 3-25 West Doe Liquids Hub; PE/manual scope basis pending package RFQ or brief. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | PE definition of slop service plus 3-25 DBM/package-structure analog; no package-folder brief.md, DOCX, or PDF scope source found. |

## Basic Scope

Supply one slop storage tank for off-spec condensate or contaminated hydrocarbon liquids requiring segregation from on-spec condensate product.

## Major Included Equipment

One API 650 modified atmospheric slop storage tank, likely TK-9130-2, with tank appurtenances, connections to relevant drain/recycle/truck-out systems, and standard tank instrumentation.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: Slop is understood as off-spec condensate product or contaminated hydrocarbon liquid, including material contaminated with water, treating chemicals, or other hydrocarbons that make it unsuitable for on-spec product storage/export. Expected sources may include off-spec condensate, tank drains, KO drum pump-out, VRU/scrubber liquids, treating-unit drains, and other contaminated hydrocarbon liquids. Final source list, disposition path, and tank design basis require process confirmation. |
| --- |

# 26020-03-PT-19-005 - Tanks, Sour Condensate

| Location / Status | 3-25 West Doe Liquids Hub tank farm; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-03-PT-RFQ-19-005 - Sour Conde Tanks.docx |

## Basic Scope

Item No. 1: Two (2) 3800 bbl Sour Inlet Condensate Storage Tanks (TK-9110-2, TK-9120-2). Process function: Sour C5+ Condensate Storage Tanks.

## Major Included Equipment

Item No. 1: Two (2) 3800 bbl Sour Inlet Condensate Storage Tanks (TK-9110-2, TK-9120-2); Design & fabrication to modified API 650; Non-insulated, blanket gas system per API 2000; Sour service (H2S present), NACE compliant; Internal coating on floors, walls, roofs (Devchem 253); Each tank to include PVRV (for vacuum or modulating pressure relief), EPRV (designed for emergency relief), VRU header connection; Fill Limit: Maximum fill 90% shutdown; Fill Rate: Tank nozzles sized so that plant design capacity can fill a single tank

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Foundations, mounting tanks at site, electrical/instrumentation, platforms, staircase etc. Capacity/design throughput: Design Flow: 27,606 kg/h / 919 Am3/d for Item No. 1; Design Flow: 94,940 kg/h / 3187 Am3/d for Item No. 2. Operating conditions: Pressure: Atmospheric; Temperature: Ambient for Item No. 1; Temperature: 5 °C (min) & 40 °C (max) for Item No. 2. # 26020-03-PT-19-006 - Tanks, Condensate

| Location / Status | 3-25 West Doe Liquids Hub tank farm; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-03-PT-RFQ-19-006 - Conde Tanks.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply Four (4) 3800 bbl Condensate Product Storage Tanks. Process function: C5+ Condensate Storage Tanks.

## Major Included Equipment

Four (4) 3800 bbl Condensate Product Storage Tanks; Design & fabrication to modified API 650; Non-insulated atmospheric tanks, a recycle may be required to maintain a certain temperature during winter, blanket gas system per API 2000; Internal coating on floors, walls, roof (Devchem 253); Each tank to include PVRV (for vacuum or modulating pressure relief), EPRV (designed for single worst-case relief), VRU header connection, blanket gas connection, tank.; Fill Limit: Maximum fill 90% shutdown; Fill Rate: Tank nozzles sized so that plant design capacity can fill a single tank

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Foundations, mounting tanks at site, electrical/instrumentation, platforms, staircase etc. Capacity/design throughput: 94,940 kg/h / 3,187 Am3/d (Preliminary Design conditions). Operating conditions: Pressure: Atmospheric (ambient); Temperature: 0 °C (min) & 40 °C (max). Design conditions: Pressure:32 oz test pressure; Temperature: -40 °C (min) & 60 °C (max). |

# 26020-03-PT-23-001 - Condensate Truck Loading Stations

| Location / Status | Managed in 26020-03 3-25 Liquids Hub project; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | RFQ/Bid Docs/26020-03-PT-RFQ-23-001_Truck_Load_stn_R0.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply Two truck loading and unloading stations, each capable of loading two trucks simultaneously (2x2). Process function: Sweet Dehydrated Condensate from the Condensate Storage Tanks is pumped by the Truck Loading pumps to the Truck Loading stations, metered, and fills the atmospheric condensate truck.

## Major Included Equipment

Truck Loading/Unloading Stations; Quantity: 2 loading station each capable of loading 2 trucks simultaneously (2x2); Basket strainer to filter our any solids contaminants; Sureflow 0300BF300SS; 316SS; c/w Mesh Screen; Emergency Shut Down Valve (ESDV); Flow transmitters

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: Shipping packages to site, installation on piles, tie-in piping, electrical connections. Mounting platform; and stairs etc. Capacity/design throughput: -. Operating conditions: See design conditions. Design conditions: Design rate per station (loading or unloading): 103 m3/h; Total Condensate Truck Loading Header flow (4 lines): 415 m3/h. |

# 26020-03-PT-27-001 - Hydrogen Peroxide Sweetening Unit

| Location / Status | 3-25 West Doe Liquids Hub; vetted package scope basis. |
| --- | --- |
| Source Basis | Bid Docs/Budgetary/26020-03-PT-RFQ-27-001-H202_Sweet_Unit.docx |

## Basic Scope

Supply 1 sour water hydrogen peroxide treatment package consisting of:; Hydrogen Peroxide Pumps – chemical injection pumps; Hydrogen Peroxide Reactors; Static Mixer. Process function: Sour water is sent to the static mixer and then to the Hydrogen Peroxide Reactors for treatment. Hydrogen Peroxide is pumped in from the hydrogen peroxide tank by the hydrogen peroxide pumps. This treated water is then sent to produced water storage tanks. (see attached PFD).

## Major Included Equipment

400 BBL Hydrogen Peroxide Storage Tank; Hydrogen Peroxide Pumps – chemical injection pumps (Vendor to design); Static Mixer (Vendor to Design); Hydrogen Peroxide Reactors (Vendor to design); Additional equipment shown in PFD.; Self-framing building to be erected at site.

| Scope Notes / Open Items: By others: interconnecting piping, DCS integration, foundations, electrical supply to MCC. Capacity/design throughput: Capacity of the treatment package is 24,154 BBL/D.; Tank Capacity is 400 BBL; Pump Capacity is TBC (Vendor to design). Driver: All pumps driven by 575V/3PH/60Hz motors. Starting method to be DOL or VFD. To include local control (H-O-A or On-Off switch. Electric motor fed from 600V MCC. Operating conditions: Sour water temperature is 9 C.; Sour water pressure is 340.54 kPag.; Sour water flow rate is 160 m3/h (24154 BBL/D). Design conditions: Design conditions TBC; Ambient temperature is: -40 Min., +35 Max. (°C),; Hydrogen peroxide to be supplied from onsite tanks. |
| --- |
